# Test inventory

This inventory has one entry per test module or standalone FreeCAD acceptance script, plus a generated entry for each individual `test_*` function or method. Each entry states its goal and why the coverage is useful. Update the module-level entries when modules or standalone scripts change; run `python tools/generate_test_inventory.py` after test additions, removals, or renames to refresh the individual-case section.

## Unit, property, and contract tests

- `tests/test_agent_context_contract.py` — **Goal:** Verify agent guidance uses canonical live state, avoids duplicate documentation, and separates historical plans from current implementation status. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_agent_observation_bundle.py` — **Goal:** Verify observation bundles include the required repository, task, source, and validation context. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_anny_avatar_backend.py` — **Goal:** Check Anny morph discovery, measurement-unit conversion, optimizer target partitioning, pose-parameter normalization, breast projection, and hip-delta localization. **Why useful:** Catches upstream API drift and geometry changes that could corrupt rig index space or move unrelated body regions during fitting.
- `tests/test_artifact_budget.py` — **Goal:** Check artifact inventories and aggregate upload sizes against the configured budget. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_assertions.py` — **Goal:** Check shared assertion helpers preserve caller-specific failure messages in pytest output. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_avatar_fitting.py` — **Goal:** Exercise avatar measurement fitting, target controls, and geometry changes at their intended authority boundary. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_gui_contract.py` — **Goal:** Check measurement groups, generated property names, provider choices, and the pose-editor entry point against their model/command authorities. **Why useful:** Prevents duplicate GUI state and controls that diverge from persisted avatar parameters.
- `tests/test_avatar_hierarchical_pose.py` — **Goal:** Check hierarchical joint transforms, parent-child propagation, and rig landmark movement. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_arm_pose.py` — **Goal:** Check arm-angle estimation and source arm-chain weights, including the geometric fallback when weights are unavailable. **Why useful:** Prevents sparse or misindexed MakeHuman weights from distorting the arm silhouette during pose fitting.
- `tests/test_avatar_model.py` — **Goal:** Validate avatar parameters, defaults, normalized controls, and stable serialization. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_orientation.py` — **Goal:** Check mannequin orientation and coordinate transforms preserve the expected forward and vertical axes. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_pose_fidelity.py` — **Goal:** Verify pose updates produce the expected joint motion and silhouette without corrupting rig identity. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_pose_geometry.py` — **Goal:** Check pose transforms move intended landmarks and preserve finite, indexed mesh geometry. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_pose_gui_contract.py` — **Goal:** Check pose-editor controls and callbacks match the canonical pose model. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_property_mapping.py` — **Goal:** Verify measurement, shape, and pose property names are generated from canonical model fields and metadata. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_provider.py` — **Goal:** Verify that the MakeHuman and imported-FreeCAD-geometry providers implement the same surface/collision contract and reject unsupported provider modes. **Why useful:** Protects fitting and simulation from invalid tessellation and alias-only APIs.
- `tests/test_avatar_shoulder_pose.py` — **Goal:** Check shoulder rotations propagate correctly through arms and preserve expected joint positions. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_avatar_skeleton_pose.py` — **Goal:** Check rotation validation/mirroring, canonical JSON round-trips, pose persistence, landmark transforms, transform composition/inversion, and weighted FK propagation. **Why useful:** Protects the shared pose representation used by the viewport editor and garment fitting.
- `tests/test_avatar_standing_pose.py` — **Goal:** Verify preset defaults, rejection of unsupported avatar schemas, consistency between mesh-generation entry points, and expected pose-induced silhouette changes. **Why useful:** Prevents silent schema migration and catches regressions in the mannequin's authored FK geometry.
- `tests/test_avatar_visual_sanity.py` — **Goal:** Detect non-finite, degenerate, misoriented, or implausible avatar mesh output. **Why useful:** Protects avatar fitting and persisted shape/pose controls from rig-index or parameter-mapping regressions.
- `tests/test_backend_authority.py` — **Goal:** Ensure domain operations use authoritative document and model state instead of stale derived copies. **Why useful:** Rejects malformed or stale state at its owning boundary before downstream geometry or simulation operations consume it.
- `tests/test_boundary_validation_expansion.py` — **Goal:** Verify invalid external inputs are rejected before changing persistent state or reaching geometry code. **Why useful:** Rejects malformed or stale state at its owning boundary before downstream geometry or simulation operations consume it.
- `tests/test_canonical_python_validation_contract.py` — **Goal:** Keep CI routed through the single canonical Python validation contract. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_changed_python_types.py` — **Goal:** Check edited Python sources satisfy the enforced annotation and type-boundary rules. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_ci_configuration.py` — **Goal:** Check workflow gates, artifact provenance, timeouts, runner routing, and cleanup policies remain coherent. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.
- `tests/test_ci_ruff_command_validation.py` — **Goal:** Verify CI runs the configured strict Ruff checks read-only and does not auto-rewrite source. **Why useful:** Prevents command-registration drift, stale selection state, and unbound controls in interactive FreeCAD workflows.
- `tests/test_cloth_diagnostics.py` — **Goal:** Check simulation diagnostic values describe solver, mesh, and contact state accurately. **Why useful:** Catches incorrect solver inputs, collision/contact results, and stale diagnostic evidence before full FreeCAD acceptance runs.
- `tests/test_cloth_solver.py` — **Goal:** Exercise solver stepping, constraints, finite-state behavior, and deterministic cloth updates. **Why useful:** Catches incorrect solver inputs, collision/contact results, and stale diagnostic evidence before full FreeCAD acceptance runs.
- `tests/test_collision_surface.py` — **Goal:** Validate immutable collision surfaces, finite geometry and triangle-index checks, thickness updates, and deterministic center calculation. **Why useful:** Protects the neutral geometry contract shared by avatar, fitting, and simulation adapters.
- `tests/test_command_adapter.py` — **Goal:** Check command adapters translate UI inputs into canonical service calls with predictable errors. **Why useful:** Prevents command-registration drift, stale selection state, and unbound controls in interactive FreeCAD workflows.
- `tests/test_core.py` — **Goal:** Exercise core pattern and seam value objects, validation rules, and deterministic geometry primitives. **Why useful:** Rejects malformed or stale state at its owning boundary before downstream geometry or simulation operations consume it.
- `tests/test_derived_geometry.py` — **Goal:** Check derived geometry preserves source-edge identity and consistent geometric output. **Why useful:** Protects edge identity, geometry, and topology so sewing and exports cannot silently diverge from authored boundaries.
- `tests/test_documentation_contract.py` — **Goal:** Verify the documentation checker enforces symbol docstrings and Google-style parameter, return, and exception sections. **Why useful:** Keeps validation gates trustworthy and prevents repository-policy regressions from allowing incomplete results through review.

## Standalone backend probes

- `tests/drape_backend_micro.py` — **Goal:** Exercise the PBD drape backend through a compact native solver probe. **Why useful:** Gives a focused backend smoke test separate from the broader FreeCAD GUI acceptance scenarios.

## Shared test support (not independently executed tests)

- `tests/support/__init__.py` — **Goal:** Provide the init fixture/helper used by multiple tests. **Why useful:** Avoids duplicated setup and keeps host input/geometry behavior consistent across acceptance cases.
- `tests/support/assertions.py` — **Goal:** Shared test assertion, substring, and non-None helpers. **Why useful:** Keeps test failure reporting consistent and avoids duplicating one-line require implementations.
- `tests/support/freecad_arc_geometry.py` — **Goal:** Provide the freecad arc geometry fixture/helper used by multiple tests. **Why useful:** Avoids duplicated setup and keeps host input/geometry behavior consistent across acceptance cases.
- `tests/support/freecad_input.py`
- `tests/support/closed_mesh_fixtures.py` — **Goal:** Provide one canonical closed-cube triangle topology for geometry tests. **Why useful:** Prevents copied face indices from diverging between geometry, avatar, and collision tests. — **Goal:** Provide the freecad input fixture/helper used by multiple tests. **Why useful:** Avoids duplicated setup and keeps host input/geometry behavior consistent across acceptance cases.
- `tests/support/freecad_bootstrap.py` — **Goal:** Normalize repository import paths for standalone FreeCAD acceptance scripts. **Why useful:** Keeps acceptance harnesses from copying environment setup and prevents root-path differences from changing imported modules.
- `tests/support/freecad_mesh_geometry.py` — **Goal:** Share vectorized top-edge pin selection across FreeCAD visual and simulation acceptance scripts. **Why useful:** Keeps boundary-index mapping and opposite-corner checks consistent instead of maintaining duplicate geometric logic.
- `tests/support/pbd_contact_helpers.py` — **Goal:** Share vectorized measurements, checkpoint capture, case-record assembly, structural rung validation, and manifest writing between the avatar and cube PBD ladders. **Why useful:** Keeps both diagnostics on one evidence schema and prevents duplicate acceptance logic from diverging.

- `tests/test_workbench_registration.py::test_workbench_command_groups_do_not_overlap` — **Goal:** Prevent command identifiers from colliding across registered workbenches. **Why useful:** Prevents GUI callbacks, command registration, and interactive state from drifting away from the canonical model. - `tests/test_workbench_registration.py::test_root_loader_delegates_registration_to_shared_bootstrap` — **Goal:** Ensure the classic FreeCAD loader delegates workbench registration. **Why useful:** Prevents GUI callbacks, command registration, and interactive state from drifting away from the canonical model.
